### [63] [Polynomial-Time MIMO Detection at the Maximum-Likelihood Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19405)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 使用堆叠式四舍五入后MMSE再单比特搜索即可在与ML相同的SNR阈值(≈2log N)下，以多项式时间实现精确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解在随机高斯线性估计框架下，预测的SNR界限是否能够在多项式时间内被实现。

Method: 采用先对线性MMSE估计进行四舍五入，再进行单比特极升投影搜索的策略。

Result: 在ρ≥2log N的情况下，算法以O(N^3)运算实现零失败概率；在ρ低于该阈值时，即使ML检测也会失败。

Conclusion: 本文证明在正方形高斯二值MIMO模型中，使用多项式时间算法即可在与最优ML检测相同的SNR阈值下实现精确块恢复。

Abstract: We prove that exact block recovery in the square Gaussian binary MIMO model can be achieved in polynomial time at the same first-order SNR threshold as exhaustive maximum-likelihood detection. Specifically, for $y=\sqrt{ρ/N}Hx^\star+w, \; x^\star\in\{\pm1\}^N,$ and independent standard Gaussian $H\in\mathbb R^{N\times N}$ and $w$, rounded linear MMSE followed by steepest single-bit descent recovers $x^\star$ with failure probability tending to zero, uniformly over every transmitted word and every $ρ\ge2\log N$, using $O(N^3)$ unit-cost exact-real arithmetic operations. The model is a special case of Gaussian random linear estimation, for which AMP state evolution and replica/MMSE formulas rigorously characterize fixed-parameter normalized performance. Those results predict the same $2\log N$ scale, but do not by themselves yield an all-coordinate guarantee in the dimension-dependent regime considered here. To the best of our knowledge, no prior work gives polynomial-time exact block recovery at the ML boundary for this setting; the closest prior square-system theorem, for the box relaxation, has first-order threshold $4\log N$. The proof places the rounded LMMSE estimate at sublinear Hamming distance from the truth, and then establishes, uniformly over every error set the local search can visit, that some wrong bit offers a quantified cost decrease while an objective barrier confines the search path. Conversely, if $0<ρ\le2\log N-\log\log N-s_N$ with $s_N\to\infty$ and $s_N=o(\log N)$, then a one-bit neighbor beats the transmitted word with probability tending to one, so even ML detection fails. Therefore, the statistical and polynomial-time exact-recovery thresholds coincide to first order in the stated arithmetic model.

</details>


### [64] [Binary Deletion Channel Capacity to Within One Hundredth of a Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19412)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本研究将二进制删失信道容量逼近误差压至每比特0.0095比特以内，实现了前所未有的精准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制删失信道容量长期未知，现有可实现率与对称界限仍相距甚远；本文致力于通过系统化的数值与解析手段逼近真正的容量。

Method: 采用状态源约简、有限不等式、有限状态输入构造、输出熵估计及删失映射计数等技术，并结合全局数值验证与解析比较，形成了上下 bounds 的计算流程。

Result: 在所有删失概率下，计算出的上下限之间的距离不超过0.0095比特，平均误差0.006522比特；下限在渐进编码意义下与容量相差不超过0.019比特。

Conclusion: 本文通过计算机辅助方法，给出了二进制删失信道容量的上下界逼近，并证实了在所有删失概率下误差不超过每传输比特0.0095比特，平均误差0.006522比特，从而大幅缩小了容量的不确定范围。

Abstract: The exact capacity of the binary deletion channel remains unknown despite decades of work on achievable rates and converse bounds. We establish a computer-assisted approximation whose error is below $0.0095$ bits per transmitted bit, uniformly over all deletion probabilities. The mean certified error bound, with uniform weighting of deletion probability, is below $0.006522$. The estimate is the midpoint of explicit lower and upper bounds. For the converse, a stationary-source reduction is combined with finite inequalities covering every allowed input configuration. Two constructions control the unobserved input beyond a finite window: one uses a common outside survivor sequence and bounds omitted deletion patterns, while the other cancels an entropy term to make outside probabilities enter linearly. For the lower bound, finite-state inputs combine output-entropy estimates with selected disjoint counts of compatible deletion masks; independent-run inputs retain additional uncertainty about output-run boundaries. Directed numerical checks establish the finite inequalities. An analytic comparison between deletion probabilities then extends the pointwise bounds over the entire parameter range. The lower endpoint supplies rates within $0.019$ bits of capacity in the asymptotic coding sense. We give the derivations, recorded computational costs, and complete numerical inputs and programs needed to verify the result.

</details>


### [65] [Source Entropy-Guided Adaptive Transmission for Communication-Driven Multi-View Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19457)
*Mingjie Yang,Guangming Liang,Dongzhu Liu,Lei Zhang,Xiaonan Liu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: - 融合源熵估计与比特预算，动态选择传输方式
- 基于信息瓶颈的ADE-MI避免交替优化
- 在WidAr3.0数据集上验证了显著性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通信资源受限条件下，如何通过通信驱动的多视角感知实现有效的数据上传与边缘推理？

Method: 构建基于多输出高斯过程的通信间隔与包触发CSI熵的解析关系，依据可用比特预算选择原始数据或任务导向传输；对任务导向方案采用信息瓶颈原理，分解为自适应分布式编码与多视角推理（ADE-MI）

Result: 实验证明熵解析上界与数值估算一致，ADE-MI在相同比特预算下优于基准方案且在时变信道下进一步提升识别准确率

Conclusion: 该自适应框架通过对源熵的实时估计，实现了通信与感知的耦合优化，有效提升了多视角CSI手势识别性能

Abstract: Communication-driven multi-view sensing relies on routine communication transmissions for sensing acquisition, while the resulting sensing data at distributed devices must be uploaded to an edge server under limited communication resources. This creates a unique coupling between sensing acquisition and edge inference: the communication interval determines the source information, whereas the uplink condition determines how much information can be delivered to the server for sensing inference. To account for this coupling, we propose a source entropy-guided adaptive transmission framework. Specifically, we characterize the entropy of packet-triggered channel state information (CSI) as a function of the communication interval using a multi-output Gaussian process. The resulting analytical bound is compared with the available bit budget, determined by the transmission rate and latency requirement, to select between original-data and task-oriented transmission. For task-oriented transmission, we formulate the communication-constrained inference problem based on the information bottleneck and decompose it into adaptive distributed encoding and multi-view inference (ADE-MI), which avoids alternating optimization between the devices and the edge server. Experiments on the Widar3.0 multi-view CSI gesture recognition dataset show that the analytical bound closely follows the normalizing-flow numerical estimate, while ADE-MI outperforms task-oriented benchmarks under the same bit budget and the proposed framework further improves recognition accuracy under time-varying channels.

</details>


### [66] [Polynomially larger deletion codes by linear hashing of substring counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19493)
*Eyal En Gad*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 用随机哈希子串计数与两色染色构造 t 删除码，冗余系数从 4 降至 2t-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改善自 1965 年以来未变的主导系数 4，逼近已知下界系数 2。

Method: 利用随机线性哈希子串计数得到标签类，再用两色染色分离仍具有相同标签的混淆字。

Result: 提供比先前最佳上界更优的冗余系数，并说明构造中标签数几乎比直接方法少 $n$ 倍。

Conclusion: 二删码冗余可压至 $3\log_2 n + O(\log_2 \log_2 n)$，并推广至 $t\ge2$ deletions 取得 $(2t-1)\log_2 n + O_t(\log_2 \log_2 n)$。

Abstract: We show that binary codes of length $n$ correcting two deletions exist with redundancy $3\log_2n+O(\log_2\log_2n)$. The previous best upper bound had leading coefficient $4$, unchanged since 1965, while the best known lower bound has coefficient $2$. More generally, codes correcting $t\ge2$ deletions exist with redundancy $(2t-1)\log_2n+O_t(\log_2\log_2n)$, improving the coefficient $2t$. We extract a code from one label class of a random linear hash of substring counts, with about $n$ times fewer labels than a direct construction. Confusable words that still share a label are separated by a two-colouring after discarding the words in components with odd cycles, and these are few because an odd cycle forces the edits along it to overlap.

</details>


### [67] [A generalization of the map $χ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19548)
*Xiutao Feng,Qiang Wang,Jingyi Yu,Anpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文将代数度2的平移不变置换归纳完备，为轻量级密码学提供了新的设计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轻量级加密算法中，低代数度的平移不变置换被广泛使用；对其全面表述有助于设计更高安全性的密码组件。

Method: 采用组合代数与有限域分析方法，对映射 χ_n 的结构和固定点、阶数展开了系统性推导，并利用符号计数技术推导出必要且充分条件。

Result: 提出并证明了一系列关于代数度为 2 的平移不变排列的分类定理，并给出相应的构造公式与存在条件。

Conclusion: 该论文通过对 χ_n 映射的推广，完整刻画了所有代数度为 2 的平移不变排列，填补了轻量级密码学中该类映射理论的空白。

Abstract: The mapping $ χ_n:\mathbb{F}_2^n \to \mathbb{F}_2^n$ defined by $y=χ_n(x)$ with $y_i = x_i + x_{i+1}x_{i+2} + x_{i+2}$, where the indices are computed modulo $n$, has been widely studied for its application in lightweight cryptography. In this paper, we generalize this mapping and completely characterize all these shift-invariant permutations of algebraic degree $2$.

</details>


### [68] [Online Material-Labeled Environment Reconstruction via Bayesian Multipath Attribution for Low-Altitude ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19819)
*Meihui Liu,Shu Sun,Ruifeng Gao,Qiuming Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 利用贝叶斯虚拟锚地点计算多径归属和材料，在线推断材料标签化城市地图，实验显示高精度达到93.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低空集成感知与通信（ISAC）中缺乏基于材料的环境重建，强调材料标签化地图在传播场景中的重要性。

Method: 提出统一的在线概率框架，将每个反射面视为虚拟锚点（VA），完成贝叶斯VA定位、多径归属以及材料推断；利用显式漫射模型计算软归属概率，并递归融合生成材料后验。

Result: 在代表性城市街道的射线追踪仿真中，相较于无归属基线，显著提升；单一UAV轨迹最终材料准确率达93.75%，且VA定位保持精确。

Conclusion: 该方法可在复杂多建筑场景下从无线多径观测中实现材料标记化环境重建，为基于传播的通道预测与无人机网络提供物理基础。

Abstract: Environment reconstruction for low-altitude integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) has largely focused on geometry-centric maps, overlooking material-dependent propagation effects. Material-labeled reconstruction is therefore a key step toward propagation-aware mapping, enabling more physically grounded channel prediction and uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) networking. However, constructing such maps from wireless multipath observations is challenging in outdoor multi-building scenarios because multipath components (MPCs) from different facades are mixed, path-to-facade attribution is uncertain, and UAV measurements arrive sequentially under time-varying observation geometries. To address these challenges, we propose a unified online probabilistic framework that represents each reflecting facade as a virtual anchor (VA) and couples Bayesian VA localization, multipath attribution, and material inference. The Bayesian front end estimates facade-level geometry and computes soft MPC-to-VA attribution probabilities using a speculardiffuse likelihood model, thereby accounting for both dominant specular paths and diffuse surface-interacted components. These attribution probabilities are used to construct attribution-aware MPC representations, which are aggregated in a VA-centric manner and mapped by a material inference network to facadelevel material evidence. The resulting evidence is recursively fused through an online Bayesian update to produce stable material posteriors and material-labeled environment maps. Ray-tracing simulations in a representative urban street scenario show that the proposed method substantially outperforms a no-attribution baseline, achieves 93.75% final facade-level material accuracy on a held-out UAV trajectory, and maintains accurate VA-based facade localization.

</details>


### [69] [Equivalence Between Nested Gibbs Measures and Log-Linear Combinations of Gibbs Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19988)
*Yaiza Bermudez,Samir M. Perlaza,Iñaki Esnaola*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究三种 Gibbs 测度运算，发现可以通过参数使嵌套与对数组合等价，应用于联邦学习可实现一次聚合与全局训练相同效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对 Gibbs 测度的可控变换，并寻找在统计学习尤其联邦学习中简化模型聚合的理论基础。

Method: 定义三种运算：重正化、对数线性组合与测度嵌套，并证明其结果仍为 Gibbs 测度，且对应特定的相对熵正则化优化。

Result: 证明存在参数集，使得嵌套操作与对数线性组合产生相同的 Gibbs 测度，并在一拍联邦学习场景下实现与全局数据训练相同性能。

Conclusion: 通过参数调控，Gibbs 概率测度的嵌套与对数线性组合在优化问题上等价。

Abstract: In this paper, three operations on Gibbs probability measures are studied. The first operation, often referred to as renormalization, takes one Gibbs probability measure and generates a new Gibbs measure by normalizing a power of its density. This normalization has a twofold effect: it changes the regularization factor and concentrates the support within a subset of the original support. Interestingly, these effects can be independently controlled by different parameters. The second operation consists of a normalized log-linear combination of the densities of Gibbs probability measures. The third operation takes two Gibbs probability measures and changes the reference measure of the latter with the former. Hence, the former is said to be "nested" within the latter, yielding a new Gibbs probability measure. The resulting measures from the second and third operations are also Gibbs probability measures and are shown, respectively, to solve optimization problems involving the expectations of linear combinations of the objective functions of the given measures, subject to a relative entropy regularization. These optimization problems differ exclusively in the coefficients of the linear combinations. This leads to the conclusion that there exists a set of parameters for which nesting one Gibbs probability measure into another has the same effect as log-linearly combining them. These operations have relevant applications in statistical learning. As an example, a one-shot federated learning system in which clients send their locally trained Gibbs algorithms to the server for log-linear combination is shown to achieve the same performance as a Gibbs algorithm trained upon the aggregation of all local training datasets.

</details>


### [70] [Tri-Hybrid Beamforming Design for Large-Scale MIMO ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20092)
*Tianyu Fang,Mengyuan Ma,Markku Juntti,Inkyu Lee,Joonhyuk Kang,Nhan Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: Tri-HBF通过优化实现能效明显提高，通信速度和感知略降，优于传统HBF或单DMA配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大规模天线阵列中能量消耗与成本问题，提出基于三融合{三重混合}射频结构的低成本动态超材料天线（DMA）与传统混合波束成形（HBF）相结合的Tri-HBF架构，并探讨其在集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中的应用。

Method: 构建加权多目标优化问题，平衡通信总速率与感知互信息，加入总发射功率与Tri-HBF物理约束；基于问题结构设计低复杂度迭代算法，闭式更新求解非凸优化。

Result: 数值仿真表明，Tri-HBF架构在相同功率下可大幅提升能效（EE），仅导致通信总速率和感知互信息轻微下降。

Conclusion: Tri-HBF在大规模天线阵列中实现了能量效率与成本的双重提升，满足ISAC系统对高效通信与精确感知的需求。

Abstract: Tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures have been proposed as a promising solution for enabling energy-efficient communications systems in large-scale antenna arrays by replacing conventional antenna arrays in hybrid beamforming (HBF) systems with low-cost dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs). In this work, we investigate beamforming design for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) systems based on tri-HBF architectures, with the objective of jointly enhancing the communications sum rate and sensing sum mutual information. Specifically, we formulate a weighted multi-objective optimization problem that balances communications throughput and sensing mutual information, subject to a total transmit power consumption constraint and the physical limitations inherent to tri-HBF architectures. By exploiting the problem structure, we develop an efficient iterative algorithm with closed-form updates to solve the non-convex optimization problem with low complexity. Numerical results are provided to evaluate the performance of the proposed tri-HBF architecture in various setups. The results demonstrate that the proposed tri-HBF architecture can achieve significant improvement in energy efficiency (EE) compared to the conventional hybrid MIMO and DMA-only configurations, at the cost of minor degradations in sum rate and sensing mutual information.

</details>


### [71] [LEO Satellite Internet of Things: Architecture, Technology, and On-Orbit Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20165)
*Ming Ying,Xiaoming Chen,Qiao Qi,Yichao Xu,Jiajun Pan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了 6G LEO 卫星 IoT 的双维系统架构，评估了三项关键技术，并通过验证平台验证了其可行性，为未来研究指明方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现全球覆盖、无处不在的 6G IoT 需要将 LEO 卫星与地面网络无缝集成，需解决上行连通性、下行鲁棒性和星间链路可靠性。

Method: 构建二维系统架构，分别针对上行、下行和星际链路评估三项技术，并实现了验证平台进行实机测试。

Result: 通过实验验证，提出的技术在提升接入效率、信号质量和网络可靠性方面表现显著，验证平台证明其真实运行可行。

Conclusion: 提出基于双维系统架构的 6G LEO 卫星 IoT 方案，验证了三项关键技术（上行随机接入、多波束深度学习预编码、分布式协同路由）与验证平台的可行性，为未来 LEO IoT 的实现提供了技术路线图。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are poised to become a cornerstone of the sixth-generation (6G) Internet of Things (IoT), providing truly global coverage and ubiquitous connectivity. This article presents a holistic two-dimensional system architecture for 6G LEO satellite IoT that incorporates composition and functional perspectives to facilitate the seamless integration of LEO satellites and terrestrial networks. Building upon this architecture, we evaluate three pivotal enabling technologies targeting the uplink, downlink, and inter-satellite links (ISLs). Specifically, we analyze massive grant-free random access for efficient uplink connectivity, investigate deep learning-based multibeam precoding for robust downlink transmission, and examine distributed cooperative routing for resilient ISL data delivery. Furthermore, we present an on-orbit verification platform that validates the real-world feasibility and performance of the proposed solutions. Finally, we outline key open challenges and future research directions to guide the realization of future LEO satellite IoT.

</details>


### [72] [Some MDS and ACD codes over commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 (Revision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20190)
*Jon-Lark Kim,Marvin Olavides,Young Gun Roe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过约简映射把$I_{p}$上的ACD码与$\mathbb{F}_{p}$上的LCD码联系起来，并用此关系对$I_{2}$、$I_{3}$中的最优码进行分类，得到的最优码是MDS码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限环$I_{p}$（尤其是$I_{2}$、$I_{3}$）上的加法性互补赎码（ACD码），填补该领域缺乏系统性分类与最优码构造的空白。

Method: 构造约简映射，将$I_{p}$中的ACD码映射到对应的有限域上，进而利用LCD码的性质进行分析与分类；对$I_{2}$、$I_{3}$分别给出最优码的具体构造。

Result: 在$n=1,2,3$（完全）以及$n=4,5$（部分）取值下，给出了$I_{2}$上最优ACD码，均为MDS；在$n=1,2$（完全）以及$n=3$（部分）取值下，给出了$I_{3}$上最优ACD码。通用化到任意素数$p$的情况，证明了ACD码与$p$-原子LCD码之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 通过从$I_{p}$到$\mathbb{F}_{p}$的约简映射建立了ACD码与$p$-原子LCD码之间的关系，并利用此关系对$I_{2}$和$I_{3}$中最优ACD码进行分类；这些最优码均为MDS码。

Abstract: There are eleven finite rings of order $p^{2}$ denoted by $A_p$ to $K_p$ in alphabetical order. In particular, we consider $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$ which are commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 defined by generators and relations as \[I_{p}=\left\langle a,b\mid pa=pb=0,\:a^{2}=b,\:ab=0\right\rangle\] for $p=2, 3,$ respectively. Alahmadi et al. studied codes over these rings. In this paper, we study additive complementary dual (ACD) codes over the rings $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$. We show relations between ACD codes over $I_{2}$ and binary linear complementary dual (LCD) codes using a reduction map from $I_{2}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{2}$, and between ACD codes over $I_{3}$ and ternary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{3}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{3}$. Using the first relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{2}$ with the highest minimum distances for $n=1, 2, 3$ and partially for $n=4, 5$. It turns out that they are maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. Using the second relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{3}$ with the highest minimum Lee distances for $n=1, 2$ and partially for $n=3$. We generalize the two relations into a relation between ACD codes over $I_{p}$ and $p$-ary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{p}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{p}$.
  This is a correction of the paper published in Advances in Mathematics of Communications, Volume 24, pages 61-76, 2026. In particular, we corrected the statements of Theorems 3.5, 4.7, 4.8, and their proofs.

</details>


### [73] [Conflict-Free Color-Clustered Sequential Belief-Propagation Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20236)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Remi A. Chou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 改进RL‑S引入颜色聚类并行化，降低调度复杂度，保持误码率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP在QLDPC码中受短环、退化与收敛失效限制；RL‑S虽改进但并行度受限。

Method: 构造VN冲突图，基于同色VN的无重叠检查邻域实现并行更新，并利用训练好的Q‑表选取种子VN。

Result: 在[[288,12,18]]双变量自行车码、偏析通道实验中，改进解码器实现了与原RL‑S相近的BER，并将调度决策从288个VN降至11个颜色类。

Conclusion: 引入冲突无关的颜色聚类扩展提升了RL‑S BP解码器的并行度，同时保持了接近原始RL‑S的误码性能。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes is attractive due to its low complexity and low latency, but it is often limited by short cycles, degeneracy, and convergence failures. Reinforcement-learning-based sequential BP decoding (RL-S) improves BP by learning a syndrome-dependent variable-node (VN) update order, but its VN-by-VN schedule has limited within-iteration parallelism. In this paper, we propose a conflict-free color-clustered extension of RL-S. We construct a VN conflict graph in which two VNs are adjacent if they share an X-type or Z-type check, and color this graph so that same-color VNs have disjoint check neighborhoods. This also prevents VNs from the same Tanner 4- or 6-cycle from being updated simultaneously. During decoding, the trained VN-level Q-table selects a seed VN, and all remaining VNs with the same color are updated in parallel using the same pre-batch messages. For the [[288,12,18]] bivariate-bicycle code over the depolarizing channel, our proposed decoder achieves block-error-rate performance close to VN-level RL-S while reducing the scheduling decisions from 288 VNs to 11 color classes per BP iteration.

</details>


### [74] [A Mirror Vanishing Band for Weight Distributions of Binary Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20344)
*Xianmang He*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文用非最小向量的支撑不交性质在 2d 的另一侧得到新的消失区带，从而把二进制线性码的非零权重数上界从 n-d+1-v 缩小了 2t，提供了更紧的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的 Chen–Xie 证明仅给出了 2d 以上的消失区带，本文希望通过第三条性质在 2d 下面再得到对应区带，以获得更精确、不依赖于上界的权重分布信息。

Method: 采用 Ashikhmin–Barg 第三条性质，分析非最小代码单词的支撑分解，得到 A_{d+1}=…=A_{d+t}=0 时 A_{w}=0 (w∈[2d+1,2d+t]) 的结果；随后结合 Chen–Xie 的正侧区带，求得整体非零权重计数上界。

Result: 若 k≥n-2d+1 且 A_{d+1}=…=A_{d+t}=0，则 A_w=0 对所有 w∈[2d+1,2d+t]。于是该码的非零权重数最多为 n-d-v-2t+1，比原来的 n-d+1-v 上界提高了 2t。

Conclusion: 通过利用非最小向量的支撑不交分解，本文在二进制线性码中在 2d 另一侧构造了镜像消失区带，从而改进了 Chen–Xie 对零权重数量的上界。

Abstract: Chen and Xie recently proved, using the Ashikhmin--Barg lemma on minimal vectors, that every binary linear $[n,k,d]$ code with $k=n-2d+2+v$ ($v\ge 0$) has no codewords of weight in the interval $[2d-v,\,2d-1]$. Their argument uses two of the five basic properties of minimal vectors established by Ashikhmin and Barg (1998). In this note we utilize the third property, the disjoint-support decomposition of non-minimal codewords in binary codes, to generate a \emph{mirror} vanishing band on the other side of $2d$: if $A_{d+1}=\cdots=A_{d+t}=0$ for some $t\ge 1$ and $k\ge n-2d+1$, then $A_w=0$ for all $w\in[2d+1,\,2d+t]$. Combining the two bands, the number of nonzero weights of such a code is at most $n-d-v-2t+1$, improving the Chen--Xie bound $n-d+1-v$ by $2t$.

</details>


### [75] [Norm-One Torus Decompositions and Decoding of Gashkov-Sidel'nikov Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20402)
*Minjia Shi,Shitao Li,Yuhong Xia,Tor Helleseth,Ferruh Ozbudak*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 作者证明了 Gashkov-Sidel'nikov 码中奇偶校验列标签为 \mathcal T，得到覆盖半径3并给出完整最大似然解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确 Gashkov-Sidel'nikov 码的符号校验列标签，分解错误纠正问题为最小错误重量与余数领导两步，并建立完整的最大似然解码方案。

Method: 通过将符号权重与量子表示及二次字符相关联，利用 Weil bounds 进行二次字符求和，构造每个语法体系的余数领导。

Result: 给出最低错误权重与余数权重分布、覆盖半径3，并构造所有余数重量为3的余数领导，实现完整的最大似然解码器。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了 Gashkov-Sidel'nikov 码中的签名奇偶校验列标签集为 \mathcal T，并给出了完整的最大似然解码器实现；覆盖半径为3。

Abstract: Let $q=3^m$, let $K=\mathbb F_{q^2}$, and let \[\mathcal T=\{x\in K^*:\operatorname{N}_{K/\mathbb F_q}(x)=1\}.\] For both cyclic and constacyclic Gashkov-Sidel'nikov codes, we show that the set of signed parity-check column labels is precisely $\mathcal T$. Consequently, the decoding problem separates into two stages: determining the minimum error weight associated with a syndrome $S$ and constructing an error vector attaining this minimum. We identify the former quantity with the minimum additive length of $S$ with respect to $\mathcal T$ and determine it exactly by the norm and the quadratic character of $\mathbb F_q$. We also determine the complete coset-weight distribution and recover the known covering radius $3$. For the constructive part, we use quadratic-character sums and Weil bounds to construct a coset leader for every syndrome of coset weight three. The resulting procedures give complete maximum-likelihood decoders.

</details>


### [76] [Maximum Entropy Probability Distributions on Spheres with Fixed Mean Busemann Function and Holomorphic-Information-Geometric Model of Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20410)
*Vladimir Jacimovic*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究了球面上的最大熵分布，并将其与复向量空间的Bergman球及RKHS理论联系，揭示了认知学习与最大熵原理的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索限能球面上的极大熵分布及其在认知建模中的应用。

Method: 通过求解约束熵最大化问题得到分布族，并利用再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）理论将信息流形与Bergman球联系起来。

Result: 得出了在能量层面上独特的MaxEnt分布族，证明信息流形同构于Bergman球，并展示了RKHS中的最小认知努力原理与球面边界概率的对偶性。

Conclusion: 在球面上构造了固定Busemann函数下的最大熵概率分布，并将其与复向量空间中的Bergman球相对应，证明了最小认知努力与最大熵原理的对偶关系。

Abstract: In the first half of the paper we revisit the question regarding MaxEnt probability distributions on spheres. We derive families of MaxEnt distributions on spheres in real and complex vector spaces with fixed expected Busemann function (energy). As a particular case, we deduce sub-families on canonical energy levels where inverse temperature equals the dimension of the sphere. In the second part we focus on the information manifold of canonical MaxEnt distributions on the sphere in the complex vector space. This manifold is isomorphic to the Bergman ball. We introduce the reproducing kernel on this manifold and use the RKHS theory to elaborate the model of cognition. In particular, we state the principle of minimal cognitive effort in RKHS and demonstrate its dual relationship with the MaxEnt principle for probability distributions on the boundary sphere.

</details>


### [77] [Galois Hulls of Generalized Roth-Lempel Codes and Their Applications to EAQECCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20453)
*Xuefei Wu,Qi Liu,Yingchun Chen,Haiyan Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 通过Lagrange系数+列乘子策略，构造多种GRL码，获取E.A.Q.E.C.C.、Hermitian LCD码，具备固定Galois极帽维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有特定Galois极帽维数的GRL码，以便构造EAQECC。

Method: 通过规范化拉格朗日系数和列乘子选择，基于乘法余类、迹纤维、加法子空间余类和混合加法－乘法纤维，给出六种显式构造。

Result: 在扩展大小s=2,3时得到MDS和AMDS GRL码，具有所需Galois极帽维数；相应地得到EAQECC，维数、纠错容量、纠错距离均给出。零维极帽得到Hermitian LCD GRL码及相应的最大纠纠码。

Conclusion: 提供了一种统一建模方法，形成了多类GRL码，在EAQECC领域实现了高维度、低纠错资源、高最小距离的量子码。

Abstract: The dimension of a Galois hull is an important parameter in the construction of entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes. In this paper, we study generalized Roth-Lempel (GRL) codes with prescribed Galois hull dimensions over finite fields of arbitrary characteristic. We develop a common construction method based on normalized Lagrange coefficients and suitable choices of column multipliers, and obtain six explicit constructions from multiplicative cosets, trace fibers, additive-subspace cosets, and mixed additive--multiplicative fibers. For extension sizes $s=2$ and $s=3$, we construct MDS and AMDS GRL codes with Galois hulls of specific dimensions. The resulting codes provide EAQECCs with explicit dimension, entanglement consumption, and relatively large minimum distance. Taking zero-dimensional hulls also gives Hermitian LCD GRL codes and the corresponding maximally entangled EAQECCs.

</details>

### [78] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建了一个意图驱动的双阶段优化框架，先用意图对齐的奖励提升多样性，再用查询级信用分配提升质量，实验证明效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要生成既有用又能覆盖不同用户意图的查询建议集，以提升用户参与度。

Method: 1) 意图驱动的多样性模型：构建与意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并利用意图感知多样性奖励优化意图覆盖；2) 查询级信用分配：将查询质量信号映射到个体查询标记，同时在整个建议集共享斑块级多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上，结合在线 A/B 测试和离线评估，实验显示该框架在点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖方面都有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过双阶段优化有效提升生成查询建议的质量与多样性，在生产环境中显著提升点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖度。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [79] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 层级课程学习+多线程缓存大幅压缩LLM，BERT/GPT‑2显存/时间↓50%；在LLaMA/Qwen亦优于剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型语言模型训练与部署中显著降低显存占用与训练时耗，是提升计算成本效益的关键。

Method: 将整个模型按层分块，采用课程学习顺序从易到难逐层迁移知识；结合理论误差累积分析，并使用多线程特征缓存解决层间特征失配，提升GPU利用率。

Result: 实验验证在BERT与GPT‑2上，显存与训练时长均降低50%以上；在LLaMA及Qwen系列模型上，在相同训练时长下优于其他剪枝方法并拥有更小显存占用。

Conclusion: 层级自适应学习在LLM压缩中显著加速收敛并稳定知识迁移，最终实现模型尺寸显著压缩同时保持性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [80] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSBP通过分片块级并行降低通信开销并提升吞吐量，使长上下文训练更快、更内存友好，显著加速多种模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于位置的上下文并行（CP）受限于分布式注意力通信和激活内存，难以扩展到更长的上下文长度。

Method: 结合块级并行化（BP）与上下文分片，单个GPU仅处理其对应的损坏块，同时将共享清洁序列分片到不同GPU，避免了冗余通信与内存复制。

Result: 在16张H200 GPU上，CSBP相较最佳基线的吞吐量提升1.18–1.45倍；在16张H200 GPU上，整体模型加速达1.61倍；在8张H100 GPU上，DFlash2模型的加速达7.59倍，验证模型在各种指标上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过引入上下文分片块并行化（CSBP），大幅提升了长上下文训练的吞吐量，同时保持或降低峰值HBM使用，并在多种硬件与模型上显著加速训练过程。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [81] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 两种随机近似技术使谱共聚类在高维文本数据上更快：投影更稳健，采样仅适用于稠密矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类依赖完整SVD，导致在高维、稀疏词文档矩阵上计算成本过高。

Method: ①使用随机投影实现随机SVD；②结合部分SVD与元素级随机采样。

Result: 两种方法均低于全SVD的计算时间；随机投影方法更通用可靠，而采样法在稠密矩阵中效果较好，对稀疏文本收益有限。

Conclusion: 依据数据结构选择随机近似，可显著提升谱共聚类的计算效率。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [82] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RF‑CNN把无线射频混频器当作卷积单元，轻量化、能源极低地完成深度CNN推理，适用于无器件扩展的边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备缺乏深度CNN所需的计算能力，传统加速器会增加尺寸、重量、功耗和成本，影响SWaP-C。

Method: 将多通道卷积映射到频率调制符号上，利用无线射频混频器（频率相乘）直接在频域完成卷积；权重通过空中无线方式送达，混频器一次性完成所有乘加。

Result: 在无线信号与图像分类、可控图像生成等任务上，RF‑CNN成功推理多达2,640万参数、9层 CNN，性能接近全精度；每次乘加能耗仅为0.72 fJ，较传统数字处理器低两个数量级。

Conclusion: RF‑CNN证明可利用现有通信硬件实现低功耗、高效AI推理，利用已有无线网络基础设施即可向数十亿边缘设备提供尖端AI功能。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [83] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过DMFT，发现学习可将RNN从混沌变为稳定；临界学习速率和反馈强度决定这一转变，理论与仿真吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释在学习阶段RNN如何抑制混沌并产生任务特定的可控动力学，为理论指导网络训练提供框架。

Method: 建立非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），推导双时间相关系数并分析学习速率与临界反馈强度之间的关系，表征从混沌到稳定的分岔过程。

Result: 理论预测了学习过程中的反馈强度演化、临界时间以及网络输出随时间的变化，数值模拟与理论结果保持高度一致。

Conclusion: 学习过程中，递归神经网络的反馈机制使系统从混沌状态过渡到可控的稳定行为，DMFT提供了定量描述和预测输出演化的工具。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [84] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedHGP 通过共享全局组件、Client‑specific 偏差和本地残差三层结构，结合稀疏诱导变量与联邦变分推断，实现高效隐私联邦概率建模，应用证明其在机械监测与空气质量建模中的卓越效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理高度异质化分布的客户端数据时，如何在保持数据本地化的前提下实现概率回归与分类。

Method: 使用个性化联邦层级高斯过程（pFedHGP）：引入共享全局组件、共享核结构的客户端特异偏差及灵活的本地残差，通过稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断，仅同步低维统计。

Result: 在 13.77% 标记周期下完美分类压机错误；在联邦空气质量建模中无需集中 station‑level 时序即可恢复地理区块；提供完整预测分布以支持不确定性决策。

Conclusion: pFedHGP 通过层级结构兼顾全局共享与个性化细节，显著提升多客户端概率建模性能，同时保证数据隐私。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [85] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发探针引导，无需额外推理前向，提升扩散模型性能并揭示自引导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决在推理阶段如何提高扩散模型的生成质量而无需额外前向传播的需求。

Method: 构造探针引导（probe guidance）：利用现有扩散模型冻结的内部状态产生引导信号，实现与强模型相似的动力学。

Result: 在连续扩散语言模型上实现无条件生成的新SOTA；在1.7B模型上在多项选择问答基准上持续提升。

Conclusion: 探针引导既是提升扩散语言模型的实用方法，也阐明了自引导机制的本质，并揭示弱模型源自低熵训练区域是成功的关键。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [86] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [87] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将注意力投影限制在 Stiefel 流形并用 Riemannian Adam 优化，可获得更优的几何稳定性与泛化性能，尤其在提升对相等变换的敏感度方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在注意力机制中将查询与键投影约束于 Stiefel流形，并在该流形上使用 Riemannian Adam 进行优化，旨在提升几何一致性、避免权重衰减导致的收缩现象，并提升对相等变换的等变性。

Method: 使用每帧仅 1 个标量二阶矩的 Riemannian Adam，步骤受信赖域限制，采用极性重投影；证明该更新是嵌入度量下的最陡下降，且与梯度尺度无关、条件良好、 O(d) 等变。进一步指出在 Stiefel 流形上权重衰减的 Riemannian 梯度为零，因而不会破坏学习到的几何。

Result: 在模块算术“grokking”任务上单次实验 20,000 轮验证准确率 97.0%（基线 61.1%）；在 CIFAR‑10 补丁实验中提升 8.98pp，且随着数据增多差距进一步扩大。Ablation 证实提升源自无尺度步长，而非投影或等变性。负面结果表明“gauge removal”无法解释该方法的效果。

Conclusion: Riemannian Adam 配合 Stiefel 约束提供了稳定、等变、无尺度步长的注意力投影优化；权重衰减不影响几何，且步长的尺度自由化是主导提升因素。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [88] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在西班牙巴拿马\n弱小数据集上，使用可解释朴素贝叶斯模型，I‑309/CCL1被认定为最具预测增益的特征，适用于认知障碍预测，未来需外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本临床表格数据需要可解释机器学习；深度学习不切实际，而集成模型难以解释；统计显著性不一定意味着预测效用。

Method: 使用泄漏安全的阈值似然贝叶斯/分类朴素贝叶斯分类器；在每一折训练中将连续预测变量离散化为监督的卡方衍生状态，收入通过类别似然纳入；全部数据相关步骤在重复分层10折交叉验证（30次重复）内完成。

Result: 基线ROC‑AUC为0.630±0.017，I‑309提升AUC 0.110，所有重复均p<0.05；在预设主分析中p=0.0018，20次随机划分中中位p=0.0011；在18候选标记中，I‑309在冻结划分上Benjamini‑Hochberg调整后的q=0.032，在85%随机划分满足q<0.05，其他标记无可靠增量。

Conclusion: I‑309/CCL1被鉴定为可解释的增量特征，对认知障碍的表格预测具有显著且稳健的预测价值，需进一步外部验证。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [89] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [90] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RSIQL 在离线目标条件 RL 中引入中间奖励刺激，解决了奖励延迟与估计误差问题，保持简单策略，实验上已显示出优于 IQL 并具竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线、高稀疏奖励、长时 horizon 的目标条件 RL 环境中，目标完成信息往往仅在轨迹末端显现，导致早期决策与最终奖励的时间耦合松散；同时，离线价值估计误差会在延迟奖励传播过程中累积放大，导致“目标导向价值”比本地估计误差仅差微小，进而破坏学习。

Method: Reward Stimulation Implicit Q‑Learning（RSIQL）使用辅助的目标条件价值函数检索预计朝向目标取得进展的中间状态，并在这些状态施加额外奖励，以此提供更早的训练监督。该方法不需要层次化的子目标策略，整体保持一层非层次策略网络。

Result: 在 D4RL 及 OGBench 的基准实验中，RSIQL 相较于基线的目标条件 IQL 平均提升了性能，并且与档层次化离线目标条件方法的效果相适。

Conclusion: RSIQL通过在离散的在线轨迹中添加中间奖励信号，有效缓解了离线强化学习中延迟奖励和估计误差导致的目标价值衰减问题，保持了简单的平面策略结构；实验表明它在D4RL和OGBench基准上平均优于传统的IQL，并与层次化方法竞争力相当。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [91] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [92] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAM优化器能有效提升Raman光谱细菌分类准确率，减少预处理需求，推动快速耐药检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌素耐药性预计到2050年每年将导致1000万死亡，资源匮乏地区受影响最大。对Raman光谱算法的不足——在多样化患者群体下泛化差且需繁琐的预处理——需要解决。

Method: 采用Sharpness-Aware Minimization（SAM）优化器提升模型对多样化临床细菌分类任务的泛化能力。

Result: 在单一次切分中，SAM相较Adam提升最高10.5%的准确率；在所有切分上平均提升2.7%。

Conclusion: SAM显著提升Raman光谱的AI诊断性能，为抗菌素耐药性快速检测的临床应用打开新途径。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [93] [Robust Federated Q-Learning with Almost No Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20174)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Robust Fed-Q 通过鲁棒统计与混合Q‑学习，克服了少数欺诈代理导致的干扰，既实现了与理想无攻击环境相当的采样效率，又显著降低了通信需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在存在少量攻击者时，联邦强化学习是否能够获得采样效率提升，并提供对抗性的鲁棒解决方案。

Method: 结合模型基础和无模型Q‑学习，并引入中位数-均值（median‑of‑means）鲁棒统计方法，以抵御欺诈代理；在每轮中聚合局部估计并对失败样本进行拒绝。

Result: 证明在高概率下（1）Robust Fed‑Q 可在无穷大样本下收敛至最优值；（2）在有限时间内实现近最优采样率，并仅需$	ilde{O}(1)$轮通信即可满足这两项保证。

Conclusion: 在带有少数欺诈代理的联邦强化学习环境中，Robust Fed-Q 能够在无限样本极限下保证精确收敛，并在有限样本期内实现与无欺诈情况相当的速度；其通信需求仅为每个阶段$	ilde{O}(1)$轮，解决了联邦学习中的通信瓶颈。

Abstract: We consider a federated reinforcement learning setting involving $M$ agents, all of whom interact with a common Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents exchange information via a central server to learn the optimal value function. Our goal is to understand to what extent one can hope for collaborative sample-complexity speedups in such a setting, when a small fraction of the agents are adversarial and can act arbitrarily. To that end, we propose Robust Fed-Q}, a federated Q-learning algorithm that blends ideas from both model-based and model-free RL, along with the median-of-means device from robust statistics. We prove that despite corruption, with high-probability, Robust Fed-Q (i) guarantees exact convergence to the optimal value function in the limit of infinite samples, and (ii) enjoys near-optimal finite-time rates that benefit from collaboration. In addition, our approach requires just $\tilde{O}(1)$ rounds of communication to achieve each of the above guarantees, a feature of independent interest in FL where communication is the major bottleneck.

</details>


### [94] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 借助环境知识提升神经网络的农业N2O排放预测准确性和可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量观测缺乏且N2O排放涉及复杂交互，单纯深度学习缺乏可解释性且泛化差

Method: 将领域知识（肥料扩散、土壤呼吸、水孔隙度）嵌入农业信息神经网络中，形成混合神经-机理框架，并在CNN、LSTM、Transformer上进行评估

Result: KAINN在多季节、多输入配置下比纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN降低RMSE、MAE、提升R²，学习到的接口参数更平滑且物理一致，不确定性降低

Conclusion: KAINN实现了对农田中N2O排放的更准确预测，提升了模型的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [95] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过线性球面代理实现BO大幅加速 (>100x)，兼具速度与性能优势，让贝叶斯优化重新成为生成管线的可选策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在大规模生成-筛选流水线中存在顺序决策导致的计算瓶颈，尤其当虚拟筛选成本较低时更为突出。

Method: 在球面高维潜变量空间上使用线性代理模型，并利用球对称性推导近似闭式求解线性回归与采集函数，使得代理建模与采集问题可在闭式或近似闭式下完成。

Result: 在分子与图像生成基准测试中，所提方法实现了至少100倍的速度提升，同时在性能上与现有最先进基线持平或略有超越。

Conclusion: 该方法将贝叶斯优化在生成-筛选流水线中的应用变得可行，可作为传统流程的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [96] [COIN-GP: Cooperative Online Learning in Networked Distributed Systems with Partial Measurements via Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20598)
*Zewen Yang,Xiaobing Dai,Zhenxiao Yin,Hang Zhao,Zhijun Li,C. C. Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于观测器+分布式GP的协作学习框架，能在部分观测下准确估计状态与动力学，误差可上界，实验结果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅能观测系统部分状态的场景下，传统方法难以同时估计系统状态与未知动力学。

Method: 对分布式网络中的各节点采用在线分布式高斯过程回归与观测器协同工作，设计一个新的数据采集策略，并通过理论推导给出误差上界。

Result: 实验模拟验证了该框架在误差上界、数据效率和估计精度上的优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出了基于观测器的动态协作学习框架，并证明其在分布式传感器网络中能够在部分状态观测下同步估计系统状态与未知动力学，且误差可被严格上界；实验表明该方法优于现有分布式GP方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the problem of jointly estimating the system states and partially unknown dynamics within distributed sensor-equipped networks, particularly in scenarios where only partial state observations are available. To address this issue, we propose an observer-based dynamic cooperative learning framework incorporating online distributed Gaussian Process (GP) regression, which enables accurate estimation despite incomplete in measurements and deficient GP models. In addition, a novel data collection strategy is introduced, with theoretical conditions ensuring feasible data acquisition. Moreover, we also derive an error upper bound encompassing state estimation and model estimation, leveraging the deterministic error bounds of GPs. Empirical simulations demonstrate the superiority of our approach compared to existing distributed GP-based methods.

</details>


### [97] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当候选数相同时，使用大批量一次生成比多次小批量生成在能耗和延迟上更优，评估指标应包含生成调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在多候选推理中，候选数与系统成本之间的关系以及更合理的调度策略。

Method: 在Phi-3-mini和Qwen2.5-1.5B模型上，用不同的生成调度（1x8、2x4、4x2、8x1）对同一候选数（N=8）进行实验，测量延迟、吞吐量、GPU‑小时和粗能源消耗。

Result: 相同候选数下，单次批量生成显著降低了能耗（4.64–4.86倍）和P95延迟（5.77–6.12倍）与连续单候选调用相比；该结论在不同GPU和任务上均成立。

Conclusion: 候选数只描述算子执行量，生成调度方案会显著影响系统成本，尽量采用批量调用以降低能耗和延迟。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts. As expected, increasing N from 1 to 8 improves accuracy by 8.4 percentage points for Phi-3-mini and 18.4 points for Qwen2.5-1.5B. However, accuracy alone does not show the systems cost of using a larger candidate budget. We therefore fix N = 8 and compare four generation schedules: 1x8, 2x4, 4x2, and 8x1, where axb denotes a generation calls with b candidates per call. We measure latency, throughput, GPU-hours, and gross GPU-device energy while keeping the total candidate count fixed. On A100 GPUs, eight serial calls use 4.64-4.86x as much gross GPU-device energy and have 5.77-6.12x the P95 latency of one batched call with eight candidates. The same pattern appears across three independently scheduled A100 nodes per model and in short-output SciQ/V100 experiments. These results show that candidate count alone is not enough to describe the systems cost of multi-candidate test-time scaling. When candidates are independent and memory allows it, fewer generation calls with larger batch sizes are more efficient. Evaluations should therefore report not only candidate count and accuracy, but also generation schedule and GPU-level systems metrics.

</details>


### [98] [LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19521)
*Aras Kavuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较了三种 Recurrent‑Depth Transformer 变体，发现只有带有有界门控记忆的 LSTM‑UT 能同时提升深度外推和延迟回忆的性能，指出门控记忆是重复计算和记忆检索的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Recurrent‑Depth Transformers 在信息保持方式上存在差异，如何在相同的共享计算框架下实现更好的深度外推与记忆检索，成为提升模型有效性的核心问题。

Method: 对三种变体（Block Universal Transformer、CoTFormer 以及 LSTM-UT）在规则30细胞自动机和延迟回忆任务上进行比较，并通过干预当前状态与缓存来分析 CoTFormer 失效的机制；实验评估了每种模型在未见深度和延迟请求下的准确性与鲁棒性。

Result: Block Universal Transformer 在深度外推上表现超过 CoTFormer，但精度会随深度增长而降低；CoTFormer 的失效主要源于当前状态与缓存的交互，修正状态可暂时恢复准确度，但缓存记忆会破坏这种恢复。在延迟回忆任务中，虽然 BUT 无直接访问过去状态，却仍赢过 CoTFormer；CoTFormer 在选择缓存表示时不可靠。LSTM‑UT 在深度外推和延迟回忆两个方面都优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过将联合门控记忆引入通用变换器（LSTM-UT），改善了在深度外推与延迟回忆任务中的表现，证明了有界门控记忆是重复计算和后期检索的有效归纳偏置。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. We compare a Block Universal Transformer (BUT), which carries only its current hidden state; CoTFormer, which also retains an expanding attention cache; and a new LSTM Universal Transformer (LSTM-UT) with bounded gated memory. On Rule 30 cellular automata, BUT extrapolates to unseen recurrent depths more reliably than CoTFormer, although its accuracy eventually degrades. State and cache interventions show that CoTFormer's failure depends on their interaction: correcting the current state can temporarily restore accuracy, while retained history can undermine that correction. In a delayed-recall task, BUT also outperforms CoTFormer despite lacking direct access to past states; CoTFormer does not reliably select the requested cached representation. LSTM-UT improves both depth extrapolation and delayed recall over these baselines. The results support bounded gated memory as an effective inductive bias for repeated computation and later retrieval in these tasks.

</details>


### [99] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在压缩空间构造主动子空间，CAS消除了存储全维梯度的成本，支持大规模神经网络的预测性不确定性量化，且保持了良好的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动子空间方法需要存储大量高维梯度，随着模型尺寸的增大，内存消耗迅速失控，导致在大型机器学习模型上不可行。

Method: 提出压缩主动子空间（CAS）方法：先用结构化等距嵌入将参数映射到低维压缩空间，再在此空间中构造主动子空间，从而避免在全维空间里保存梯度。

Result: 在多种规模递增的神经网络上验证，CAS大幅降低了内存需求，并在保持预测性能的同时，提供了稳健的贝叶斯不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CAS通过参数压缩实现了主动子空间方法的可扩展性，为大模型的贝叶斯推断提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [100] [FedFIbOS: Fisher Importance based Optimal Submodelling for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19559)
*Yasmeen Afzal,Jeremiah D. Deng,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedFIbOS：利用 Fisher 信息进行子模型参数筛选，在异构联邦学习中理论上可行、实验表现优异，准确率提升约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异构联邦学习中子模型参数的选择主要依据经验性重要性度量（如参数幅值），缺乏对收敛性的理论依据，且在仅部分客户端参与时会产生额外的估计误差。

Method: 提出 FedFIbOS，通过基于 Fisher 信息的准则（最小化子模型掩模误差）来进行子模型选择，并用平方梯度估计斜对角 Fisher 信息，实现了无额外优化开销的自适应参数筛选。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 与 AGNews 的病态与 Dirichlet 非IID 环境下，FedFIbOS 的准确率比现有方法提升约10%，提升幅度随异构程度增强而显著。

Conclusion: 提供了子模型选择在异构联邦学习中收敛性理论的完整框架，使得 Fisher 信息成为可靠的参数筛选指标，并验证了其在多种数据分布下的有效性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning requires clients with diverse computational capacities to collaboratively train a global model, where each client trains a capacity-constrained submodel. Existing methods select submodel parameters using heuristic importance measures---most prominently parameter magnitude---without theoretical justification for why these measures support convergence. We identify a fundamental gap: existing parameter selection criteria lack theoretical grounding in the convergence framework, partial client participation introduces additional estimation effects in the Fisher scores. We propose \textbf{FedFIbOS}: Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling for heterogeneous federated learning, using Fisher Information in a principled criterion derived from minimizing submodel masking error. %We formally establish when magnitude selection is equivalent to Fisher selection fail under non-IID heterogeneous federated learning. We theoretically formulate submodel selection through a Fisher-weighted quadratic masking surrogate and show that the raw Fisher top-$k$ rule implemented by FedFIbOS solves this surrogate under a Fisher-dominant ranking condition. The resulting method retains the convergence structure of the underlying masked federated optimization bound. Fisher scores are efficiently estimated from empirical diagonal Fisher information using squared gradients, enabling stable and adaptive parameter selection without additional optimization overhead. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and AGNews under pathological and Dirichlet non-IID settings show FedFIbOS achieves ${\approx}10\%$ higher accuracy than the state of the art, with improvements becoming more pronounced under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [101] [The Complexity Kink: A Prompt-Side Structural Complexity Index for Code-Generation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19616)
*Michael Hernandez,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 先建立预生成的六维提示复杂度指数，人工评分并四名LLM评审验证，ICC=0.872；随后对21模型生成的105k短程序进行分析。发现提示复杂度与成功率非单调，阈值因任务与框架而异。该框架为代码生成的可靠性评估提供了可操作的提前指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代码生成任务中，提示的复杂度往往与生成结果的正确性相互作用，且传统的复杂度评估往往依赖生成后输出，难以在生成前进行风险评估。为此本文欲提出一套独立于正确性的提示结构复杂度指标，帮助提前衡量风险并理解模型生成行为。

Method: 研究先构建六维结构复杂度指数（从生成角度衡量），设定单量表及六个复杂度段；\n5,000个Python提示通过人工标注完成初评；\n随后由四名LLM评审对封闭提示进行再评分，获取19,997条评分记录（22个提示已全部四分）；\n利用这些评分计算合成ICC=0.872；\n在此基础上对21种语言模型在每个提示上生成代码，共105,000条生成样本；\n进行均值池化、非单调截断分析、任务类型与框架固定效应检验、模型特异性拟合、12 365提示审核正则扩展等多层次统计方法，并对结果进行过度识别检验。

Result: 四名LLM评审的复评合成ICC达到0.872，证明评分体系具有较好的可靠性。均值池化分析指出，在提示复合分数13.75处存在非单调截断，超过该阈值模型通过率为87.6%，低于该阈值为79.9%。任务类型固定效应将截断点调整至10.75，并将成功率差距从7.6下降至2.1；框架控制将截断点移至8.50，差距反向为-3.5。模型特异性拟合显示16个模型上升、5个模型下降。365提示扩展验证了原始 5 模型估计，且仅在高分段增添了14条提示。对无法通过的生成方案，28.5%的提示复合分距>8伴随输出复杂度≤10。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套预生成测量框架，并对多模型生成结果进行可靠性区间的观测分析。通过构建六维结构复杂度指数，对5,000个Python提示进行人工评分并与四名LLM评审打分协同验证（ICC=0.872），验证了评价机制的信度。随后对21种模型生成的105,000例代码进行均值池化与分段点分析，发现提示复杂度与输出成功率呈非单调关系，且任务类型、框架等因素会显著改变阈值与差距。综合结果表明，提议的指数可作为生成前的可靠性指标，为后续模型评估与提示设计提供参考。

Abstract: Complexity measured from generated code is failure-dependent: a difficult prompt can yield a short failing program and be assigned low output complexity. We introduce a six-dimension prompt-side structural-complexity index scored before generation and kept separate from correctness. We select 5,000 Python prompts across six bands of a preliminary single-rater rubric. Four out-of-panel LLM raters rescore the locked prompts, giving 19,997 score rows; composite inter-rater reliability is ICC = 0.872 on the 4,998 prompts with all four ratings. We evaluate 21 models per prompt, yielding 105,000 generations. In the unadjusted mean-pooled analysis, pass rate has a nonmonotone breakpoint at composite 13.75, with 79.9% at or below and 87.6% above. This is not a universal failure cutoff. Task-type fixed effects shift the breakpoint to 10.75 and cut the regime gap from 7.6 to 2.1 points. A construction-frame control shifts it to 8.50 with a raw gap of -3.5 points, and neither frame alone reproduces the pooled +7.6-point change. Model-specific fits include 16 upward and five downward changes. A 365-prompt audit-clean extension matches the original five-model estimates at bins 15 and 16 but adds only 14 prompts above bin 16. Among zero-pass generations with computable Lizard complexity, 28.5% pair a prompt composite above 8 with output complexity at most 10. Human agreement is moderate and rater-dependent on a disagreement-enriched calibration set; paraphrase and cross-language rescoring preserve score ordering. Overidentification tests reject the joint restrictions on the six dimensions, so we treat the composite as an index and make no causal interpretation of the 2SLS estimates. The contribution is a pre-generation measurement framework and a bounded observational analysis of reliability regimes.

</details>


### [102] [A Policy Profile for Croissant: Refusal as a Property of the Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19640)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为 Croissant 数据集描述符增加可执行的评估规则，门式判定无误且开销低，100% 语料通过一致性验证，且呼叫者与数据两类策略互斥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本工作指出在 Croissant（基于 JSON‑LD 的机器可读数据集描述符）和其数据使用条件（DUO/ODRL）中缺乏评估流程，导致不确定的决策结果、无查证记录、以及与调用者权限的组合不明确。

Method: 提出一种可附加的‘profile’：通过针对五个关闭运算符提供完整决策程序，使门（gate）仅凭描述符即可判定是否满足条件，并记录已检查内容。随后使用两个真实语料库、改造后的 nf‑core pipeline 以及基于语法生成的语料评估该方案。

Result: 门的判定结果与本机描述符记录以及 ODRL 规则一致。实验显示在 nf‑core 实例中，额外层成本仅为 11.7 µs（相较 119 µs），并在 552 个完整决策记录中三方一致。语料库覆盖了所有运算符、拒绝类与符合性条款。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了 Croissant 描述符的完整评估语义，验证其有效性与效率。并证明调用者绑定与数据绑定策略属于互不相交的状态空间，二者不相互包含。

Abstract: Croissant is the de facto machine-readable descriptor for ML datasets: JSON-LD over schema.org. Since version 1.1 it also carries data use conditions, recommending DUO and ODRL for them. What no version specifies is how any of them is evaluated: no decision procedure, no bound on evaluation cost, no outcome for a condition an implementation cannot evaluate, no record of what was checked, and nothing on composition with caller-side authority. We supply that half. An additive profile lets a dataset declare the operations it admits and the conditions under which it admits them, over a closed set of five operators whose decision procedure is given in full, so a gate decides from the descriptor alone and records what it checked. Two corpora evaluate it and their evidence is kept apart. Three descriptors that gated a real nf-core pipeline give the deployment result: decisions from a profile document match the gate's native descriptor record for record, stripping the layer leaves a valid Croissant document, and the added cost is 11.7 $μ$s against a 119 $μ$s decision. A corpus generated from the profile's grammar gives the breadth, covering every operator, refusal class and conformance clause. Across its valid cases, 552 complete decision records agree three ways -- native descriptor, profile terms, and the same policy as ODRL in usageInfo. The carrier is therefore not the contribution; the evaluation semantics is. Finally, caller-bound and data-bound policies range over non-overlapping state spaces, so neither permit set contains the other.

</details>


### [103] [CoRe: Coherence and Relational Alignment for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19670)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Huiran Duan,Yining Liu,Zhixiang Wu,Chu Lin,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRe introduces a lightweight loss that enforces frequency coherence and low‑rank relational consistency for multivariate forecasting, outperforming existing objectives on benchmarks without adding any trainable parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pointwise loss functions (e.g., MSE) used in direct forecasting provide stable supervision but do not explicitly preserve the structure of future trajectories—temporal coherence within each variable and relational consistency across variables can be weakened.

Method: Replace pointwise supervision with two output‑space constraints: a frequency coherence loss aligning predicted and target spectra, and a low‑rank relational graph loss matching sampled pairwise differences in a target‑derived PCA subspace. No trainable parameters; applied by changing only the loss in existing backbones.

Result: On standard benchmarks, CoRe improves strong baselines, competes favorably with recent forecasting objectives, and remains robust across different backbones, datasets and hyperparameter settings.

Conclusion: CoRe is a simple, effective, model‑agnostic training objective that enhances direct multivariate forecasting by preserving temporal coherence and relational consistency in the predicted trajectories.

Abstract: Direct forecasting has become a standard paradigm for multivariate time-series forecasting because it predicts the full future horizon in a single pass. However, its training objective is often still decomposed into pointwise errors such as MSE. Such objectives provide stable supervision, but they do not explicitly preserve the structure of the future trajectory: temporal coherence within each variable and relational consistency across variables can both be weakened. We propose CoRe, a model-agnostic learning objective for direct multivariate forecasting. CoRe replaces pointwise supervision with two output-space constraints: a frequency coherence loss that aligns predicted and target spectra, and a low-rank relational graph loss that matches sampled pairwise differences in a target-derived PCA subspace. The resulting objective introduces no trainable parameters and can be applied to existing forecasting backbones by changing only the loss. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe improves strong baselines, compares favorably with recent forecasting objectives, and remains effective across different backbones, datasets, and hyperparameter settings overall consistently.

</details>


### [104] [Conservation Buys Stability and Factoring Buys Counterfactuals in Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19674)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 学习型模拟器会因长期误差和物理法则变更而失效。通过能量+辛积分保持轨道稳健；通过线性因式分解实现对无见耦合符号的迁移。两种机制可分离，实现更稳健的物理模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于经验，学习型模拟器在训练外表现出两类失败：（1）长期回放误差累积导致轨迹漂移；（2）物理参数变更但模型仍遵循训练时的规律。需要针对这两类问题提出不同的结构性解决方案。

Method: 采用能量学习模型配合辛积分器保证能量守恒与几何保真，并通过显式线性因式分解捕捉物理耦合，使模型在未见的耦合符号下仍能正确迁移。
